Also put `billiard8.onrender.com` into the Facebook app's **App Domains** (Settings -> Basic); without it Facebook answers "Can't load URL: the domain of this URL isn't included in the app's domains" (seen on the owner's phone). The Facebook app must be in *Live* mode (or the signing-in account a tester). No new secret is needed on the server. The Google consent screen must be published ("In production") for players that are not listed testers.
- Tests: `node server/test_auth_browser.js` (start redirects, verified hand-over, once-only claim, cancel, rate limit, fake Google / Facebook checkers via `GOOGLE_TOKENINFO_URL` / `FB_GRAPH_URL`) and `node tools/test_login_app.js` (the app with a fake Capacitor: whole Google / Facebook rounds with the real `/auth/done` page, cancel and retry, a closed tab, the settings window).
- **Still not verified on a real phone:** the Chrome tab, Google's / Facebook's own pages and the `royalbilliard8://auth` jump back were exercised only with fakes here (no Android device, no Google / Facebook test account).

## Quiz translations (16 languages)
Quizzes are shown to every player in their own game language. `uz` and `en` come from the bank itself
(`tools/channel_bank/*.txt`). The other 12 languages live in `tools/channel_bank/i18n/<lang>.txt`
(`Q001|question|opt1;opt2;opt3;opt4|explanation`, `=` keeps the bank option). Run
`python3 tools/build_quiz_i18n.py` to validate them and regenerate `server/quiz_i18n.json`.
`ru` has full explanations; the others have questions and options only, and show the English explanation.
A native-speaker review of the less common languages is recommended. The Telegram channel stays uz + en.

## First-visit download screen (like PUBG)
The light build (`web/`) lists every picture and sound with its size in `web/assets.json` (made by `tools/build_web.py`, served by the game server with `no-store`).
On the first visit the page shows a progress screen ("Downloading game resources (5.3 MB / 30.1 MB) 18%"), puts all files into the browser cache (`billiard8-v6`, the same one `sw.js` reads) and opens the game when done (~30 MB, once).
The next visits find everything in the cache and open at once; after an update only the new files are downloaded (the list version is kept in `localStorage.b8_assets`).
"Later" lets the player in at once, the download goes on in the background; a stalled connection (20 s with no progress) lets the player in as well. The text is in all 14 languages (small table inside the page's first script).
Nothing changes in the single-file build, in portal frames, or in browsers without cache storage. Test: `node tools/test_preload.js`.

## HD pack (sharp avatars, frames, cues, badges, icons, ball textures)
The pictures are drawn for a 1340×720 screen; a 2400×1080 phone or a 4K tablet shows them 2–3 times bigger, so small ones looked soft. The fix is a second download, like the "HD resource pack" of big mobile games:
- `tools/build_hd.py` makes a sharper copy of the small pictures (**1541 of them, 50.6 MB**: avatars 100 → 400 px, frames ×2, cues 1160 → 2320 px, badges / medals / icons ×3, ball textures 320 → 640 px) with Real-ESRGAN running on the CPU
  (`tools/hd/esrgan.py`, onnxruntime, no torch, no GPU, no credits; `pip install onnx onnxruntime pillow numpy`; about 1 hour for everything, resumable: existing files are skipped). The copies are `web/hd/<hash>.webp` (the hash is the one of `img/<hash>.<ext>`), kept in git.
  Big pictures (halls, backgrounds, tables, > 1300 px) are left alone, they are already 2–3K.
- **Not every picture may be bigger**: some are measured in pixels by the code or the CSS (table rails and pocket rings with a pixel table next to them, 9-slice `border-image` borders, `background-size:auto`, pictures drawn with `drawImage(img, x, y)` or shown with no CSS size). `tools/build_hd.py` skips them (`EXCLUDE_OWNERS`, the CSS scan `css_risky()`, and `tools/hd/exclude.txt`, 527 pictures).
  After a big UI change run `node tools/hd_risk_audit.js out.json` (`CRAWL=1` also clicks every button of every screen), add what it finds to `tools/hd/exclude.txt` and run `python3 tools/build_hd.py --prune`.
- `tools/build_web.py` lists the copies in `web/assets.json` (`hd`: `[base name, size of the HD copy]`) and keeps every picture that has one as a file (never inline), so it can be swapped. Rebuild the light build after `build_hd.py`.
- The start-up script at the top of `index.html` downloads the standard pack first, then the HD pack (its own progress: "Downloading HD graphics (x MB / y MB)") into the cache `billiard8-hd`, and reloads the page once. `sw.js` answers a request for `img/<hash>.<ext>` with the copy kept in `billiard8-hd` when there is one,
  so no page code asks for HD files: avatars become 400 px, cues 2320 px and so on. It only downloads when the browser has a service worker running (otherwise the pictures could not be used).
- A slow or metered connection (saveData, 2g/3g) and a very weak phone (< 2 GB) keep the standard pictures; Settings → Graphics → "HD graphics pack" downloads, applies or switches it off (`window.__hd`).
- Settings → Graphics has a new level "4K" (the screen's own pixels). The default level is 2K on strong devices (6 GB+, 8+ cores) and Full HD on the others; hand-drawn avatars are painted at 256 px on sharp screens; balls are painted up to 96 / 72 px, from mip-mapped textures (the HD ones on big screens).
- Tests: `node tools/test_hd.js` (first visit → HD → reload → HD pictures, second visit, Settings off/on), `node tools/hd_diff_capture.js out/ && python3 tools/hd_diff_compare.py out/` (every screen with HD off / on: nothing may move),
  `node tools/bench_perf.js` (frame cost by picture quality, HD and CPU speed).
- Hosting: the pack is ~80–100 MB per new player, so it must come from a host with free traffic. The static build that Cloudflare publishes after every push (`wrangler.jsonc`: `python3 tools/build_web.py web_site`) includes `web_site/hd/` (copied from `web/hd/`, which is committed), so the Telegram Mini App / web version gets it from Cloudflare.
  The game server on Render also serves `web/` (`/hd/<hash>.webp`), but a free Render plan has a monthly traffic limit: use the Cloudflare address in ads and in the bot.

## Lobby matches that lose the connection (freeze / "left the lobby by itself")

The owner's report: in a lobby match the game "left the lobby by itself", froze, and the other player stood frozen in the lobby while the first one kept shooting. Four causes, all fixed:

- **One handler for "match".** `Net.on` keeps a single handler per message type; the quick-match search (`findOpponent`) used to install its own `match` handler and keep it, so every later bracket match of the lobby was answered with `resign` (the player "left by itself"). There is now one router (right after `Net.on("oppBack")`): a match with `tour` goes to the lobby (`lbMatch`; a lobby this phone has already left → `resign` at once), any other goes to the search that is waiting (`searchWait`), a match nobody waits for is given up. The search also leaves the lobby on the client (`lbDrop`: the server takes the player out of the lobby when he queues), and playing a table in the middle of a running tournament is refused.
- **A bracket match that waited behind a result screen** (`LB.queued`) started only from that screen's "Continue". A 1 s timer now starts it as soon as the player is on a menu screen with no result / VS screen / pop-up in front. `lbMatch` ignores the same match twice (the server sends it again when a phone reconnects).
- **Dead connections.** A phone that changed network / came back from the background keeps a socket that looks open but is dead. The client pings (`{t:"ping"}` → `{t:"pong"}`) every ~4.5 s, after `visibilitychange` / `online` it must be answered within 5 s, and a connection silent for 15 s is dropped on purpose (`dead()` in the `Net` closure) → `Net.on("close")` → `netLost()` (in a match) or `lbResume()` (in a lobby, now also in a running tournament).
- **A drop no longer ends the match.** The server keeps the match `MATCH_GRACE` (30 s, env `MATCH_GRACE_MS`) for a phone whose socket closed: the opponent sees "Raqib ulanishini yo'qotdi — kutilmoqda…", then "Raqib qaytdi ✓"; only after the grace time he wins (`end`, reason `left`). The lobby seat is kept too (also in a running tournament; `sweepLobby` gives it up after `LOBBY_GRACE`).
  - **resume:** the phone reconnects (every 1.2 s, up to 34 s), sends `{t:"resume", room, got}`; the server puts the new socket into the old one's seat (`swapSocket` / `adoptRoom`: lobby, bracket, live match), answers `{t:"resumed", rx}` and replays what the opponent played that it missed. A finished match answers with its `end` (kept 10 min in `recentEnd`), an unknown one with `noresume` (server restarted).
  - **No shot is lost, none is played twice:** the server numbers the relayed `shot` / `state` messages (`n`, kept in `room.log` per seat) and counts what it received from each seat (`room.rx`: shot / state / result / resign); the phone drops messages with an `n` it has seen (`rxN`) and keeps what it sent (`sent`), so after `resumed.rx` it sends again only the messages the server did not get. A lobby `rejoin` in a running tournament also re-sends `match` + the opponent's log.
- Test: `node tools/test_lobby_drop.js` (2 real browsers: search then lobby match, queued match starts by itself, a dead connection, a closed connection, a phone that never returns; `MATCH_GRACE_MS=6000`).

## Delete my account (Google Play requires it)

Settings → "Hisob va saqlash" → red **Delete my account** → confirmation → `POST /api/account/delete {confirm: "DELETE"}`: the server removes the player (profile, sign-in links, friend links / requests in other players' lists, direct messages, club seat, invite links); purchase and payment-order records stay for the accounting but lose the link to the player; his socket is closed. The phone then wipes its local data and starts again as a new player. The public page `/data-deletion` (the URL for the Play Console "delete account" field) describes this and the e-mail way. Test: `node tools/test_account_delete.js`.

## Card payments: "I paid - check", late transfers, the admin's /orders

- **Check button.** The card-transfer window (and Settings → "To'lovni tekshirish") has "To'ladim — tekshirish": `POST /api/p2p/check {id?}` looks for the bank SMS again; if the money is not seen it sends the admin (`ADMIN_TG_ID`) a Telegram message with Confirm / Reject for that order (**every press** sends one - "N-marta bosdi", "oldin M marta rad etilgan" - only a double tap within 3 s, `P2P_ASK_GAP_MS`, is one message). Without `ADMIN_TG_ID` + `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` there is nobody to ask: the player is told the SMS has not come yet.
- **"Reject" means "not confirmed", not "closed".** A player may press "I paid" as a joke first and really pay later, so the admin's Reject on a card-transfer order only counts a refusal (`rej`): the window says "To'lov tasdiqlanmadi", the button works again, the order stays open (30 min, then 24 h for a late transfer), the next press asks the admin again and the bank SMS or the admin's Confirm still pays it. (A receipt order, `atm` mode, is closed by Reject as before.)
- **One price.** The window shows "Narx 12 600 so'm + 19 so'm" (the price + the small unique amount that recognises the payment). Items sold at their plain price (`FLAT_USD` on the server: the +1 tries, Golden Shot x3, Surprise Box) showed double on the shop's card button (the client added the "$1.99 minimum / +$0.50" card rule that the server skips for them: 25 300 on the button, 12 619 in the window); `FLAT_CARD_SKUS` / `cardUsdOf` in `index.html` now mirror the server.
- **What arrived is written down** (amounts and time only, never the SMS text): `db.meta.p2pn`. `GET /api/health` shows `p2p: {cards, auto, atm, key, admin, smsCount, lastSms, open}`: open it in a browser - `smsCount: 0` after a real transfer means the phone's SMS forwarder never reached `/api/p2p/notify` (check the forwarder app, the URL and `P2P_NOTIFY_KEY`).
- **Late money is accepted.** An auto order lives 30 minutes (`P2P_TTL_MS` in tests), but a transfer that arrives up to 24 hours later still pays it (also the admin's Confirm on an expired order); new amounts avoid the amounts of orders that expired in the last 24 h.
- **The admin in the bot chat:** `/orders` lists the open orders (10 newest, expired ones of the last 24 h too), each with Confirm / Reject. Use it for a payment whose SMS never came.
- **Telegram Stars balance:** `/stars` (only from `ADMIN_TG_ID`) shows the bot's Stars balance (Bot API `getMyStarBalance`) and the last 10 movements (`getStarTransactions`: who paid, payouts to Fragment).
- Tests: `node server/test_p2p.js` (mock Telegram API), `node tools/test_p2p_ui.js`.

## Online match: the opponent sees my cue, and both phones throw the same dice

- **Cue / table of the lobby.** The server built a player's profile once, when he joined the lobby, so a cue bought or equipped afterwards stayed "Boshlang'ich" for the opponent. Now the page sends `{t:"prof", cue, tbl}` whenever the equipped cue / table changes (a 1.5 s check), the server keeps it in `ws.pref`, and `startRoom` rebuilds both profiles (`mkProfile`) from the player's current data (cue, table, avatar, frame, level, club).
- **Raised butt and hit point.** The `aim` message now carries `e` (elevation 0-1) and `sx` / `sy` (where the cue hits the ball), `shot` carries `e`; the opponent's cue is drawn raised the same way and the cue ball shows the same hit point (also for spectators).
- **No more "balls in other places".** The hard-collision rule that pops a ball off the cloth used `Math.random()`: every phone rolled its own dice, so after a break the two simulations differed (measured: 13-680 px, a ball potted on one phone only) and the opponent's phone then snapped to the shooter's positions. The roll now comes from `seededRandom(match seed ^ shot number ^ the two ball numbers)` (`G.simSeed0`, `G.shotNo`, `G.simSeed`): both phones throw the same dice (measured after the change: 0 px). The authoritative `state` after every shot stays as a safety net, and a correction is drawn as a glide (`b.ox / b.oy` decay, ~0.3 s) instead of a jump.
- Tests: `node tools/test_cue_sync.js`, `node tools/test_break_sync.js` (BREAKS=n; run it on an older checkout with `B8_ROOT=` to see the old differences).

## Voice: one microphone, a remembered choice, listening without a microphone

The report: "after ~15 games the lobby voice stopped", "the microphone asks for permission again and again", "if listening is on I must hear the opponent, with listening off nothing is heard".

- **One microphone (`MicHub` in `index.html`).** The lobby room and the in-match voice used to ask the browser for a new microphone at every match (`getUserMedia` x2 per match) and to wait for the answer before the room came back - a phone that asks again (or never answers) left the room dead. Now there is ONE stream for the whole app: it is asked for once, muted with `track.enabled` (never stopped), and handed to whoever needs it; the room comes back at once after a match. It is let go only 90 s after the player has left every lobby and online match (and when he taps ✕ / hangs up), then taken again silently the next time voice starts.
- **The choice is remembered** (`localStorage b8_voice` = `{mic, mute, spkOff}`): tap 🎤 once and every later lobby / online match starts with the voice on by itself (no tap, no question); mute and speaker-off are kept as well. Switching off = ✕ in the lobby, or hanging up (tap while the call is not connected, right-click) in a match - only then the saved "microphone on" is forgotten. A match that merely ends keeps it.
- **Listening needs no microphone.** In a match: when the opponent starts talking and my speaker is on, I join as a listener at once (receive-only call, no question asked); with the speaker off nothing joins and the sound is muted; switching the speaker on while he talks joins. The same in the lobby: when somebody in the room has an open microphone and my speaker is on, I join the room as a listener. The speaker is one switch (lobby and match share it, it is remembered).
- **A dead call is rebuilt.** The lobby room starts a call again from scratch (`heal`) when it is not connected for 30 s although both want it, when the phone comes back from another app (`visibilitychange`), and the retry counters start over every 30 s; a match offer that is never answered is repeated after 10 s; the in-match signalling is handled one message after another (no two offers at once).
- The in-match voice is the same code at every online table (random table, friend challenge, lobby match): `node tools/test_voice_table.js` runs two browsers that meet at an ordinary table (no lobby) for 4 tables in a row - a listener joins and hears by himself, speaker off mutes, the saved microphone starts by itself, the browser is asked once.
- Test: `node tools/test_voice_cycles.js` (CYCLES=16): real Chromium pairs with a fake microphone - listening / speaker off / speaker on; 16 lobby matches in a row, audio flowing after every one, the voice on by itself in every match, the microphone asked for ONCE (every later question is made to hang like a phone that does not answer), the choice kept in the next lobby, ✕ kept off. Run it on an older checkout with `B8_ROOT=` to see the old behaviour (the room dead after the first match, 4 questions for 3 matches).

## Full screen: typing, the top bar, the ratings screen (owner's batch)
- **Typing in full screen no longer flashes the screen black.** The phone's own keyboard makes Android resize the window / show and hide its bars. On a touch screen in full screen (Fullscreen API or Telegram's) a text box now gets the in-game keyboard (`igk`, the one a phone held upright already used) at the bottom of the page: `igkOn()` = turned page or (full screen and coarse pointer). Its ⌨ key still hands the box to the phone's own keyboard. A computer (mouse as the main pointer) keeps its own keyboard. `igkHide` gives the box back to the phone's keyboard (`inputMode` reset). Also, `resize()` writes the canvas size only when it changed (writing the same size wipes the canvas). Test: `node tools/test_fs_keyboard.js`.
- **No band behind the top menu:** `#nav` has no background / border / shadow; the buttons and money chips float over the screen's own picture (home: the stage's extension picture), and the empty strip lets taps through.
- **Ratings screen (`#leader`) in the owner's mockup style:** ornate tabs (the active one blue with laurels), a header with a crown-and-laurel emblem, the title, "Ends in", the prizes with ribbon medals and a "Last week" gold button, laurel medals for places 1-3, framed rows (level star, flag, name + league on ONE line, coins), an 8 ball in laurels at the bottom when the list is short. The art is drawn in code (SVG data URLs, nothing to download). Rows are sized from the page's height (`--lbrow`), about ten are visible on a 915x412 phone.

## Y8 ads: what the ad network answered, and who is away
- **`/admin/ads`** (page; asks for the `ADMIN_KEY`) and `GET /api/adstat?key=ADMIN_KEY` show, per day and per ad break (`start / game-start`, `next / between-matches`, `reward / ...`), what the ad network answered (`breakStatus`: `viewed`, `dismissed`, `notReady`, `noAdPreloaded`, `frequencyCapped`, `timeout` ...) and how many were really viewed. The Y8 page reports each ad break to `POST /api/adstat` (only the Y8 build; no account needed; counted, nothing personal). So "no ad came" can be told apart from "the game never asked". When the game opens on Y8 (once per visit, not on the very first launch, not for ad-free players, not when today's 10 ads are used up) the **video gift** window comes first (see below); a short `start` ad follows only if the player says Later or no video is available. After every 2nd finished match (at most every 90 s) comes a `next` ad. Whether an interstitial is a video or a picture is decided by the ad network; a rewarded ad is always a video. Test: `node tools/test_adstat.js`.
- **A player who is away loses the match:** in an online match a player whose turn runs out 3 times in a row (`AFK_MAX`) loses it; he is warned after the 1st and 2nd ("Miss N more turn(s) and you lose"), the opponent sees "Opponent idle: n/3". One shot resets the count. Solo matches are not affected. Test: `node tools/test_afk.js`.
- **Messages in an online match:** the opponent's bubble stays 5 s (was 2.6 s), the 💬 button shows a red dot until the chat is opened, and the chat panel lists the last lines ("Name: text"). Stickers and gifts travel the same way. Test: `node tools/test_chat_net.js`.

## Which country am I playing with, and crisp in-game buttons
- **The opponent's country** (the server always sent it in the match message, the page dropped it): it is kept in the opponent object (`country`, also for lobby matches and watching) and shown as the flag next to the name plus the country's name under it in the match HUD (`drawPanel`), on the VS screen and on the result screen (`ctryHTML`), and as "flag Country · Name" in a pill a few seconds into the match. A computer opponent shows no country (🤖 AI). Test: `node tools/test_country.js`.
- **In-game buttons are the owner's mockup pictures** (`tools/art/hud_buttons_mockup.png`): `python3 tools/add_hud_buttons.py` cuts out the cue key, the cue ball (the red dot is painted out, the game draws it where the cue hits), the chat, menu and full-screen keys and a blank dark key (zoom +/-, microphone, speaker) and stores them as `IMG.hud_*` between `/*HUD_ART*/ ... /*HUD_ART_END*/` in index.html. The HTML keys use them as CSS backgrounds (sizes divided by `--uiz`, so a phone's smaller menus do not shrink the game's buttons), the canvas draws the ball and the cue key from them (`hudImg`). Do not redraw them by hand: the owner wants his picture, not an imitation.
- **Tab rows wrap:** `.subtabs` wraps to a second row when the tabs are wider than their box (the cue picker in Russian on a turned page lost its last tab "Победа").

## The home screen is the owner's mockup (top bar, five mode cards, limited-edition tile)
The owner drew the finished home screen (`tools/art/home_mockup.png`, 1672x940) and marked three parts: the top bar, the five mode cards and the LIMITED EDITION tile. All three are **cut out of his picture** (never redrawn):
- `python3 tools/add_home_art.py` (needs Pillow + numpy; `onnx` + `onnxruntime` for the sharpener, the weights come from `tools/hd/esrgan.py`'s cache) cuts every piece with a rounded-rectangle mask measured from its gold rim, takes the dark background out of the soft edges, paints out what the game writes itself (the name, the level number, the XP bar and its numbers, the money numbers, the card titles, the timer, "LIMITED EDITION") with a push-pull fill from the surroundings, sharpens each piece with Real-ESRGAN x4 (the HD pack's upscaler) and stores it at twice its natural size. Pieces: `home_pframe` (avatar frame: laurel + crown, the portrait's window is empty), `home_pplate` (profile plate), `home_pstar`, `home_ptro`, `home_club / pass / shop / cues / rank` (the five icon frames), `home_blank` (an empty frame: the "Mini" button puts its own icon in it), `home_mplate` (money plate), `home_gem / cash / coin / plus`, `home_key / fs / set` (the dark keys), `home_c_play / cups / events / mini / friend` (the cards) and `home_c_lim`. They go into index.html as `Object.assign(IMG, {...})` between `/*HOME_ART*/ ... /*HOME_ART_END*/` (a partial run `--part top|cards|c_lim,...` merges into what is there). `--sheet out.png` writes a contact sheet on a dark and a grey ground to look for fringes. The same run takes the three old painted cards out of the hero picture (`IMG.homeBg`; the untouched original is `tools/art/home_hero_orig.webp`) and writes the card positions as CSS between `/*HOME_CARDS_CSS*/ ... /*HOME_CARDS_CSS_END*/`. The scene behind the old cards is stratified (skyline reflection, neon lines, floor), so `clean_hero()` does not blur: every row of the hole is a blend of what stands left and right of it on the same row (the lines go straight on) with the left neighbourhood's fine texture laid over it; a blur / flat fill left grey patches round the smaller cards.
- **Top bar:** still 62 px high; the profile card (avatar behind the laurel frame, name, level star, XP bar with its numbers, trophy), five framed icons (Club, Pass, Shop, Cues with its `1/471` count and the purple collection-power badge, Ratings) plus the "Mini" key, one money plate (diamond / cash / coins with green + keys), full screen and settings. No labels under the icons (the mockup has none). **The profile card stands in the LEFT corner and the two keys in the RIGHT one** (the owner: "put the top one back in its place"); the profile plate grows up to 300 px and the money plate up to 440 px to fill the room, the icons stand in the middle between them (equal gaps), and on a very wide screen the bar is a centred strip of at most 1760 px so the gaps never open up. `fitNav()` still drops the least important pieces step by step when a phone is narrow; it now **skips a pass while a money chip is bumping** (a landing coin makes the chip 16% bigger for 0.2 s, which used to look like an overlap and hid the whole bar for up to 1.2 s). The two stretched plates use a 9-slice with **percentages** (`border-image-slice: 10.49% ...`), so an HD copy of the picture cannot shift the cut.
- **Cards and tile:** the five cards stand in one row (`.hmc`, ids `tPlay tTour tEvents tMini tWithFr`), 14 px apart, centred on the stage's middle line at 84% of the mockup's size (the owner: "make the middle menu smaller"; `S_CARD` in the tool), the **VIP tile** (`#hLimited`, its timer `#hLimEnds` is a child; the owner drew an arrow on the old "Limited edition" tile and asked for a VIP section there: the ribbon says **VIP** and a tap opens the VIP window through `vipOpen()`, the same window that opens at launch, on the page of the day the first time and where the player left it after that; the weekly limited cue `limitedCue()` stays in the code but is no longer on the home screen) bottom right. **The VIP window at launch** (`buildHome()`: `vipShownSession`, once per visit, 1.8 s after the home screen, waits while another window is open) now opens on the **Android app** too (the app has `NO_CARD`, which used to switch it off; the owner: "abbda ochilganda VIP oynasi ochilmayapti"); there the pack is bought for **💎** (see "VIP packs for 💎 in the app" below) and no `$` price is shown. Y8 and the other portals keep no launch window (their start has the video gift / the first-click ad); the tile works there. Test: `node tools/test_vip_tile.js` (web / app / Y8: launch window, tile label, tap, page memory, no `$` where no card works; 6 pass / 13 fail on the old code). The stage is stretched on wide-short screens (`--iky`); the cards are scaled back about their middle (`transform: scaleY(var(--iky))`), so the pictures keep the mockup's proportions and the tap area is the picture. The words stay HTML (`.hl`, `.hbtag`, `small`, `.hlim`, `#hLimEnds`), so every language is covered; `fitLabels()` shrinks a long word (German "Mit einem Freund") to the space between the Play card's two wreaths / inside the ribbon.
- Test: `node tools/test_home_art.js` (4 screen sizes + 14 languages: every picture is loaded, the top bar never overlaps itself, the numbers stand inside the profile plate, a tap on every key and card opens the right screen, the profile card is in the left corner and the keys in the right one, the icons stand in the middle (equal gaps), a bumping money chip does not squeeze the bar, the cards keep their proportions, take at most 72% of the picture and are centred, do not overlap, the words / timer / ribbon label stay inside, the 9-slice is in percentages).
- **The VIP window's icon row and the Loop window on small and turned screens** (the owner's two Telegram screenshots, Russian, page turned): (1) in the tall VIP window (`horrorOfferModal()`) the picture of the *Frame* tile lay on top of its label: the icon row `.hci` was a grid whose track grew to the picture's height (a 54 px picture sat 14 px too low in the 26 px row of the compact layout), the floral frames (`.avp.avi.avj`) carry a margin of 36% of the cell's WIDTH that the old zero lost to (it was weaker), and the compact layout shrank the inner pictures a second time (`.hsmall .hci img`). Now `.hci` is a flex row, every frame has margin 0 and ONE transform on `.avx`, sized to the real extent of the frame art (139% / 140% / 272% of the avatar box: the 54 px row gets .72 / .72 / .36, the 26 px row of the compact layout .34 / .34 / .16). (2) the Loop window (`loopModal()`, card-priced: Stars + so'm in Telegram) was taller than the window on phones (inner heights 527-557 CSS px) and the so'm button of every offer was cut off; `@media (max-height:700px)` / `(max-height:560px)` rules shrink the title, the pictures, the lettering and the buttons in two steps (the page turns every `max-height` media rule into an `html.mh<N>` class driven by the page's own height, so they work on a turned page too). Test: `node tools/test_vip_loop_fit.js` (`QUICK=1` for a short run): Loop = four offers x eight languages x Telegram stars / stars + so'm / web `$` x six screens (turned phone, landscape phones, laptop, narrow desktop): the content fits without scrolling, buttons stay inside their card and the screen; VIP = every page of the pager that has a frame x the same six screens: the frame picture stays inside its cell and off the label.

## VIP packs for 💎 in the Android app (the owner: "abbdayam olmosda sotib olaydigon qilib qoy")
The app has no card button (`NO_CARD`: Google Play wants Play Billing for real money, so the VIP window used to say "Tez orada"). Now the same pack is bought with the player's **diamonds** there (💎 are bought in the app's diamond store through Play Billing, `openDiamondStore()`; 💎 are never given free):
- **Where:** `horrorOfferModal()` (every page of the VIP pager, 18 flat pages + 64 weekly horror pages). On the web / Telegram the button is still the `$` / Stars one (`#hBuy`); on Y8 / the portals it is still "Tez orada" (no 💎 store there); **in the app** it is a green `#hBuyD` "Hammasini olish 💎 N" button (`isApp()` only).
- **Price:** `vipGems(t) = diaFor(t.usd)` = USD x `DIA_PER_USD` (50), rounded to 5 (a $6.99 pack = 350 💎). `t.usd` is the pack's list price; the card price (`cardUsd`, +$0.50) is not used for 💎.
- **Buying:** `vipBuyGems(o, t)`: already own the cue -> back to the window; not enough 💎 -> `gemShort()` (opens the diamond store); else a confirm window ("Hammasini olish · 💎 N", green price button + Close). On the confirm tap `vipPack(t)` (coins, cash, vip days, `items`: the cue, its frame `pframes`, table `tables`, avatar `avs`, `goldShots`, `sboxes`) is applied through `Net.applyGrant({kind:"purchase", gems:0, ...})` (the same function the server's grants use; `gems:0` so no 💎 are added by it) and **only after that worked** the price is taken (`profile.gems -= price`): if `applyGrant` throws, nothing is charged and the window reopens. (The pack first, the 💎 after: no line of the client ever *raises* `profile.gems` for this, which `test_economy.js` audits in the source: exactly 3 places.)
- **Test:** `node tools/test_vip_gems.js` (18 checks): the app shows the 💎 button and no `$`; the price is USD x 50 rounded to 5; the confirm window comes first and "Yopish" charges nothing; the confirm charges once (a second tap on the same button does nothing) and gives the SKU's coins / cash / VIP points, the cue (owned + equipped), frame, table, golden shots and boxes; the window then says the cue is yours; a zodiac pack also gives its avatar; too few 💎 open the diamond store and charge nothing; a throwing grant charges nothing and gives nothing (negative control: with the 💎 taken before the grant and not given back, this check fails); all 82 pages give exactly what the server's SKU gives (its card price = base + $0.50); web keeps the `$` button, Y8 keeps "Tez orada".
- **Not verified here:** real Play Billing diamonds, a real phone, Google Play review (only Chromium with a fake `Capacitor` / `NativePurchases`).

## Y8: what happens when the game opens (one ad on the first click; the "Gift" window is gone)
- **The owner (Nov 13): "when I enter, show that one ad, nothing else" - and (Nov 16, with a screenshot of the window) the "Gift" video window was still there, so it is removed.** On the **Y8 page** nothing is asked at load and **no window opens**: the **first click / tap of every visit** starts the one short `start` ad (`start / first-click`, `y8FirstFire()`), returning and new players alike; ad-free players get none. `y8Preroll()` only does something for the other builds that ask the plain `start` ad at load (Playgama / CrazyGames / GameMonetize, the Android app, Telegram with an Adsgram block). The rewarded video keys stay: Free rewards, the missions window, the x2 after a won match (all pay `VID_COINS`).
- The length and the kind (picture or video) of a **`start` / `next` ad is the ad network's choice**; on Y8 it is a short ad of about 5 s that the game cannot make longer. Only a **rewarded** ad (`reward`) is a real video of about 15-30 s, and it pays several times more per view; it needs the network to have one ready (`noAdPreloaded` = none ready for this player).
- **`VID_COINS` = 300** is what every video button pays now (Free rewards page, the missions window; it was +15, which nobody would watch an ad for; the "Gift" window that also paid it is gone). It is one constant: change it in one place. The 10 rewarded ads a day limit is unchanged (`profile.adCount`).
- The failure message of a rewarded button is honest now: "no ad available" when the network answered `notReady` / `noAdPreloaded` / `frequencyCapped` / timeout, "not watched to the end" only when the player closed it.
- `/admin/ads` shows `y8 | start | first-click | viewed` next to the rewarded ones (`y8 | reward | <place> | viewed`), with the average length in ms: about 5 000 for the short ad, 15 000-30 000 for a real video. Tests: `node tools/test_y8_build.js` (no window and no ad at load, the first click starts exactly one ad, the rewarded keys pay only when viewed, first launch, ad-free) and `node tools/test_adstat.js`.

## Weekly prizes: who gets what, where it goes, where it shows (the owner, Nov 16: "I took 1st in Uzbekistan and got no prize; do the world top 3 get paid?")
- **Weeks** start Monday 00:00 UTC. `rollWeek()` (server) ranks last week's players (`weekWon` of verified ONLINE matches: `CITY_PRIZE` of a won city match, lobby tournament prizes) and hands out **💵 cash** (an in-game currency, not coins and not real money): **world top 3: 1500 / 750 / 400**, **top 3 of every country: 400 / 200 / 100** (on top of a world prize; a country needs players with a `country`), league prizes in coins, the 3 best clubs' members 💵 20 / 10 / 5. Nobody else gets cash; a player with 0 winnings is not ranked.
- **"Haftalik yutuq" / "Выигрыш за неделю" is a ranking score, not a prize**: the coins a player won in online matches that week. They were paid when each match ended; the "450" in the recap card is that score. (The prize is the 💵 in the "Top 3 prizes" panel of the card.)
- **The prize goes to the account that took the place.** The Y8 page, the Telegram Mini App and the website are separate accounts with separate wallets (the owner plays "Ajalingman" on Y8 and "Logistika" in Telegram): Ajalingman's #1 of Uzbekistan pays Ajalingman's wallet, not Logistika's.
- **Delivery:** the grant waits in `u.grants` and is handed over ONCE in the answer of `POST /api/sync` (`applyGrant()` in the page: wallet + a toast "+400 💵 Davlat reytingi sovrini · #1 UZ"). The page now also remembers it (`profile.prizeLog`, the last 12 weekly / country prizes) and the end-of-week recap window ("Itogi nedeli", `showWeeklyResults()`) shows a green strip under the player's card: **"✅ +400 💵 Country ranking prize · #1 UZ"** - only what this device really credited (a toast can be hidden behind the start ad). Players without a prize see no strip.
- **Fixed: the first minute of a new week.** The week used to be closed only by a 60-second timer, and a `/api/sync` (or a finished online match) that came before the timer moved the player to the new week and set his `weekWon` to 0 - his last week was gone, no prize, no recap. `rollWeek()` (an idempotent string compare when nothing is due) now also runs first thing in `/api/sync` and in `addWeekly()`; at server start it already ran before the first request (Render's sleep / deploy).
- **Known weakness (not changed): one-shot delivery.** The server forgets a grant as soon as it has put it into the answer; if that answer never reaches the page (a dropped connection, the Telegram WebView suspended at that moment) the grant is lost. A fix is possible (the page confirms the ids it applied, the server keeps them until then, the page skips ids it has seen) but it touches every grant including purchases, so it needs the owner's yes.
- Tests: `node server/test_week_prizes.js` (15 checks on the real server: the prizes for world / country top 3, nobody else, the recap data, and the week change with a fake clock `tools/fake_clock.js` - test only: `node -r ./tools/fake_clock.js server/server.js` with `CLOCK_FILE`; 4 of them fail on the old server) and `node tools/test_week_prizes_ui.js` (16 checks in the real page, web / Telegram / Y8: the wallet gets the prize once, `prizeLog`, the recap strip).

## "×2 (video)": the match prize once more for a rewarded video (the owner: "you beat the opponent, and then a button to double the money you won")
- After a **won match** the result screen has an orange **`📺 ×2`** pill **right beside the prize chip `🪙 +N` under the winner** (N = the match prize; the owner: not among the menu keys - a key that popped in late shifted them and players hit "back" by mistake, on Y8 they "left the game"; it shows when the coins have landed and pulses a little; the same on every platform: `#resAr` in `index.html`, `.feeRow .dblb`). The result keys are only `❮` / share / "Play again". Tap it, watch the video (a **rewarded** ad, ~15-30 s, `reward` / `double-win` in `/admin/ads`), and the same prize is paid once more (`+N` toast, coin shower to the top bar); the "+N" coin row of the rewards list and the chip under the winner become **+2N**, the button turns into `✅ ×2` and works once. Closed half way / no video available: nothing is paid and the button works again (the same honest toast as the other video buttons). Ad-free players get it at once, without a video. It is shown only where a rewarded video works (**Y8, Telegram (Adsgram), the Android app, the portals** - **not on the plain website in Chrome: no ad network is connected there**, so neither this button nor any ad can appear until one is (for example Google AdSense for Games, which needs the owner's account): `Y8.sdk && Y8.adsReady`, the same test as the other video buttons); not after a lost match, not in lobby matches or trials (their prize is 0), not for cash 💵 / 💎.
- **Where** is one switch: `DBL_PLACES` in `index.html` (`dblHTML()` + `dblWire()` + `dblOk()`): `win: 1` is on, **off by default** (built and tested, set to 1 to turn one on): `box` (the opened mystery box, `double-box`), `daily` (a coin daily gift opens a small window instead of the toast, `double-daily`), `wheel` (coin segments), `lucky` (lucky / golden shot coin rings), `scratch` (shown on the prize line), `level` (the new-level celebration, which then stays 9 s instead of 4 s). Never doubled: tournament / cup prizes, missions, the quiz, VIP daily gifts, pass claims, referral and the other video buttons.
- **Limits:** not for a prize under `DBL_MIN` = 100 coins; at most `DBL_DAY` = 10 doubles a day for everybody (`profile.dblDay / dblN`, ad-free players too, so the free ×2 cannot double every prize); the videos share the usual 10 rewarded ads a day (`profile.adCount`), so when today's 10 videos are used up the button is not offered. A tap while another ad is playing is ignored.
- Side fixes found on the way: the level-up celebration's 16:9 picture was 35% too big on phones (its `vh / vw` are the zoomed boxes' px, `fixUnits()`), so it was cut at the top and bottom and the cash line was off the screen: it is zoomed like the other boxes there now (unchanged on a desktop); a scratch card whose prize is paid no longer wipes what is below the prize line on a later scratch; hovering an orange / red popup button no longer turns it green.
- Test: `node tools/test_double.js` (49 checks: the default is the win only, a daily gift / level-up without a button; who is offered the button (limits, ad-free, no SDK), viewed / closed / no video / retry / ad-free / another ad playing, then with every place switched on the box, daily gift, wheel (coin and cash), scratch card, lucky shot (coin ring and cash centre), level-up, and the won / lost / no-SDK result screen with real taps, the doubled coin row and chip; `SHOT=dir` saves a picture of every window).

## Typing with the in-game keyboard: the typed text is always readable (the owner: "I must read what I am typing")
- The in-game keyboard (`igk`: full screen on a touch screen, or a turned page) stands at the bottom of an upright screen. The chat panels of the lobby (`#lbChat`), of the spectators (`#specUi`) and of the world chat (`#wChat`) are fixed to the **screen**, not to the shrunk page, so their text box used to stand behind the keys. `resize()` now writes the keyboard's height (screen px) into **`--kbh`** (0 when the keyboard is hidden or the page is turned); `html.igk #lbChat, #specUi, #wChat` end at `bottom: calc(var(--kbh) / var(--uiz))`, and `html.igk #modal` gets `padding-bottom: var(--kbh)`, so a pop-up with a text box (the code to join, the name, the friend search) is centred in the strip above the keys.
- **A strip above the keys shows what is being typed** (`.pv`, `igkPv()`): the box's hint when it is empty, else the end of the text with the caret; it follows the keys, ⌫, a message that was sent (the box was emptied by a script) and a caret moved by a tap, so even a box that is out of sight or tiny can be read.
- **The phone's own keyboard in a browser** (Chrome, the owner: "the world chat is cut off behind the keys"; inside Telegram the app resizes the page itself and nothing is needed) lies *over* the page, only `visualViewport` gets shorter. `resize()` measures it as **`--osk`** (class `html.osk`, only while a text box is in use and the game's own keyboard is not up; a pinch zoom is not counted): an upright page lifts `#lbChat` / `#specUi` / `#wChat` (`bottom`) and pop-ups (`padding-bottom`) above it, a turned page narrows them from the right (its screen bottom is the page's right edge). Test: `node tools/test_kb_os.js` (the keyboard is simulated by a replaced `window.visualViewport`).
- Test: `node tools/test_kb_view.js` (25 checks; `SHOT=dir` saves pictures): the lobby and spectator panels end above the keys, a pop-up is centred above them, the strip follows the typing, the same on a turned page.

## Y8: AdX declined the page (Uzbek HTML) and the build sent Icy Tower's IDs (Y8's developer, Nov 16)
- **THE RULE (the owner, Nov 16: "never lang=uz, never forget this"; also in `CLAUDE.md`, which every session reads): the HTML of every page the game ships is English, NEVER `lang="uz"`, with no Uzbek label in it.** The source `index.html` says `lang="en"`; the builds move its Uzbek labels (the translation keys) out of the markup; `UZ_HTML=1` (the plain Uzbek page, tests only) is refused outside the temp folder; the app build fails if its HTML is not English; `node tools/test_html_lang.js` (~30 s, no browser) checks all of it - run it before every commit that touches `index.html`, after rebuilding `web/`. At run time the game still sets `document.documentElement.lang` to the player's own language (the live page, not the HTML an ad network reads).
- **What Y8 found** (the reason for `noAdPreloaded`, eCPM $0.01, "Ads are not available right now"): *"billiard_8's build page is marked and its static HTML text is Uzbek. The game switches to English at runtime, but Google judges the page from its HTML. Uzbek isn't a Google-supported publisher language, so AdX declines the in-game requests. Icy Tower (lang="en") and 8_ball_pool_elite fill normally with the same SDK setup. Fix: make the static HTML text English, and restore y8GameId "288343" / y8AppId "6ad579a6abe0343702b12ba1" (the v1.6 build sends Icy Tower's IDs). AdX fill should come back within a day or two of the new build going live."*
- **The IDs:** Royal Billiard 8 = **game `288343`, app `6ad579a6abe0343702b12ba1`** (`CONFIG.y8GameId` / `y8AppId`). `282389` / `6a9dd9da38fed6db2f1f1339` (put in on Nov 16 from the owner's message) are **Icy Tower's**: never put them back. Before changing an ID: compare it with this line and with the game's page in the Y8 developer portal.
- **The page's language:** `tools/portal_html.py` makes the raw HTML English without changing what a player sees: `<html lang="uz">` -> `lang="en"` (+ `content-language: en`); the **85 pieces of Uzbek static text** in `<body>` (menu labels, titles / placeholders / aria labels) are replaced by an invisible marker (`U+2063` + number) and their originals go into `window.__SEN` in the head; `restoreStatic()` in `index.html` puts them back **before** `collectStatic()` takes the static text for the language switch, so labels, translations and sizes are exactly as before (`document.documentElement.lang` is set to the player's language at start, as always). What stays readable in the HTML is English: the title, the `<noscript>` paragraph, the Terms / Privacy / Refund links, `VIP`, `VS`, `Mini`, `Chat`. The build fails if a letter of Uzbek text is left in the markup (`leftover()`).
- **Every build does it** (the owner, Nov 16: "did you make lang=en for Telegram, the app, Playgama too?" - the first version did the Y8 zip only): `tools/build_web.py` (so the website + Telegram `web/`, the Android app's `android/www/` and the Y8 zip) and `tools/build_crazy.py` (Playgama; the CrazyGames / GameMonetize variants too, but the owner does not use them). `UZ_HTML=1` keeps the plain Uzbek page (only the tests use it). The root `index.html` (the source, also what `server.js` falls back to when `web/` is older than it) keeps Uzbek labels: they are the keys of the translations. Remember: the website / Telegram need `web/` rebuilt + deployed on Render, the app needs a new aab, each portal needs its zip uploaded again.
- **Encoding-proof:** the `window.__SEN` script is plain ASCII (emoji and `…` are `\uXXXX` escapes) and stands right **after** `<meta charset>` (the charset is in the first 1024 bytes as before), so a host that sends no charset header can never make a label unreadable. Running the tool on a page that already went through it changes nothing.
- What this does **not** prove: that an ad network fills after it. Y8 said AdX should come back within a day or two of the new build; Playgama / CrazyGames / GameMonetize / Telegram (Adsgram) / the app (AppLovin) never said their ads depend on the page's language - for them it is a precaution, not a known fix.
- Tests: `node tools/test_html_lang.js` (the rule above, ~30 s) and `node tools/test_portal_html.js` (58 checks, ~5 min: five builds; the raw HTML of the website / Telegram / app page, the Playgama page and the Y8 zip: `lang=en`, no Uzbek label, ASCII script after the charset; the default build equals the plain page through the tool; a second pass changes nothing; and for uz / en / ru / th / tr the static labels (emoji included, the test server sends no charset), their keys, the placeholders and the home screen's text equal the plain page's; no marker left in the DOM; the IDs).

## Typing on a touch screen: the in-game keyboard in the app, in a portal's frame, in full screen - and Back never leaves a framed page (the owner, Nov 16: "the text box is behind the keyboard again; Back turns the screen black", then "wherever I write a message this keyboard comes up and the screen goes dark")
- **Why the phone's own keyboard is wrong in some places:** (1) on Y8 (and Playgama / CrazyGames / GameMonetize) the game runs in an **iframe**: `document.fullscreenElement` is `null` there even when the portal made the frame full screen, and `visualViewport` does not shrink for a frame, so the game knew neither that it was full screen nor how high the keyboard was (`--osk`): the world chat's box stood behind the keys; (2) the **Android app** is an immersive window (its bars are hidden): the phone's keyboard shows them and resizes the page, the screen flashes black; (3) in **full screen** the same.
- **Rule (`igkOn()`):** a turned page, or a **coarse pointer** (touch) and (full screen **or** `IN_FRAME` (`window.top !== window.self`) **or** the Android app (`igkApp()`) **or** a language the in-game keyboard can type completely: `IGK_LANGS` = uz, en, ru, id). So: the app, Y8 and the other portals, full screen: the in-game keyboard for every language; the website and Telegram: for uz / en / ru / id; a Turkish / Spanish / Thai ... player on the website / in Telegram keeps the phone's own keyboard (the in-game one has no accents, no Thai / Arabic / Hindi / CJK); a computer (mouse as the main pointer) keeps its own keyboard everywhere. The in-game keyboard: strip above the keys with what is typed, panels and pop-ups end above it, **⌨ hands a box to the phone's own keyboard** (for the letters it lacks).
- **Back (`popstate`)** now: hides the in-game keyboard first, then closes the world chat panel (it used to count as "home"), then pop-ups / screens / the match menu as before. At the home screen a **framed game never calls `history.back()`** (it navigates the page that holds the frame: the whole tab went blank / black); the portal has its own exit key. The website / the app keep "press twice to exit".
- Test: `node tools/test_iframe_keyboard.js` (20 checks, `SHOT=dir` saves a picture): the game in a frame like Y8's (touch: igk, box above the keys, the strip shows the typing, Back x3; mouse: own keyboard), the website in en / uz / ru (igk) and tr / th (own keyboard), the app (fake Capacitor) in en / th (igk, box above the keys). `test_fs_keyboard.js` and `test_kb_os.js` (the phone's own keyboard measured by `--osk`, now for a language igk cannot type) were adapted.
- The `<` + Y8 key at the screen's top left is **Y8's own** overlay (it covers the left of a panel's title); the game cannot move or hide it.

## Friend hub ("Do'stlar bilan o'ynash") on a phone
- The cards of this screen were too big on a 915x412 phone: the rules that shrink them stood only under `.mh520` (height <= 520 menu px), and a phone's menu is 560 px high after the 0.74 zoom. The same three rules now also stand under **`.mh560`** (4 cards per row, smaller picture, title and button). Checked at 915x412, 667x375 and a portrait phone (`tools/test_ui_fit.js` still passes).

## World chat ("Dunyo chati"): one room for every player, with the lobbies that look for players (the owner: "like the world chat of Mobile Legends")
The owner showed the Mobile Legends world chat: a small key in the menu opens one chat for players of the whole world (text, emoji, stickers); every line shows the sender's league; a player can post his lobby and next to it stands a **Join** button; it asks the lobby's owner "accept?" (✓ yes / ✗ no); only a yes puts the asking player into the lobby.
- **Where:** a small, quiet round 🌐 key on the home screen (`#hWorld`, stage position left 65 / top 458: above the bottom-left row of keys (Rewards / Friends / Invite / Watch), where the owner circled it on his phone; a little see-through). It opens the window `#wChat` (two panes, like the video): **left** the chat (each line: avatar with its frame, league badge, name, level star, flag, time; text, emoji or a sticker picture; a text box, an emoji / sticker panel with the sticker packs the player owns), **right** the list "🎱 Lobbies · N" (a card per posted lobby: table, seats `n/8`, entry fee, the host's avatar + league badge + league name, the green **Qo'shilish ➜ / Join ➜** button; "Closed" when it cannot be joined) and a button under it: "Send my lobby" (the player hosts an open lobby) or "Open a lobby" (opens the tables screen). The window closes with ✕, a tap on the dark side, or when a match starts. Not in the portal builds (`CRAZY_BUILD`: CrazyGames / Playgama / GameMonetize have their own chat rules).
- **Posting a lobby:** the host's lobby screen has a 🌐 key (`#lbWorld`) and the list has the button above (`POST /api/world/lobby`: only for an open lobby the caller hosts, with a free seat; once per 45 s for a lobby, a burst of 3 then one per 2 minutes). The post lives while the lobby is open and not running (at most 30 minutes) and shows the seats as they are now; when the lobby closes or starts it leaves the list, and **when the poster leaves and another player takes the lobby over, the card goes too** (the card is the host's; the new host can post it again). **The lobby's code is never in `GET /api/world`** (the list has an opaque `wid` only).
- **Join = a question to the host:** `POST /api/world/join {wid}` -> the server pushes `wreq` to the host's lobby socket -> a banner with ✓ / ✗ appears on the host's screen wherever he is in the menus (it is a banner over the screen, not a window) -> `POST /api/world/answer {rid, ok}`. The asking player sees "Request sent - waiting for the lobby owner" with a Cancel key and asks `GET /api/world/req?rid=` every 1.5 s. **Only on ✓ does the server hand him the lobby's code** and he joins like with a typed code (`lobbyJoin`); ✗ -> "The lobby owner declined", no answer within `WORLD_REQ_MS` (default 40 s) -> the server answers `timeout` -> "did not answer" (not the same as a decline), full / closed -> told, and a player who has a lobby of his own is asked to leave it first. One open request per player per lobby; a burst of 3 requests, then one per 8 s.
- **Rules of the room** (server): the last 150 lines are kept **in memory** (a restart of the server empties the room - nothing personal is stored), and **a line lives one hour** (`WORLD_TTL_MS`, default `3600000`; the owner: "1 soat tursin"): older lines are dropped from the room, the admin list and the report check, and `GET /api/world` answers `from` (the oldest line still alive) so an open window drops what is gone; `node server/test_world_ttl.js` (a 3-second TTL); 100 characters per line; swear words are masked (`cleanChat`); **links, `@names` and phone numbers are refused** (`WORLD_CONTACT`: no advertising, nobody lured away); the same line twice within 20 s is dropped; 3 lines per 3 s per player and 40 lines per second for the whole room; stickers only from the game's own packs (`WORLD_STK`). The window polls `GET /api/world?after=<id>` every 2.5 s **only while it is open** (plain HTTP, no extra sockets, nothing while it is closed).
- **Moderation:** tap a name or avatar -> *Report / Block / Close*. Block hides that player's lines and lobbies on this phone (`profile.wblock`, up to 200). A report goes to the admin list; **three different reporters within an hour mute the sender for 30 minutes** (`wmute`). **`/admin/world`** (asks for `ADMIN_KEY`, the same key as `/admin/ads`) lists the room's last 100 lines, the lobbies and the reports with "Jim 1 kun / Ochish" (mute 1 day / unmute) keys; the same data is `GET /api/world/admin` and `POST /api/world/mute {uid, mins}` (header `x-admin-key`). A muted player who types sees "You cannot write for now".
- Text in 14 languages (`tools/xt.txt`, 27 new lines). Not in this version (ask if wanted): language rooms, a red dot on the 🌐 key for new lines, private lines between two players (the friends' messages exist already).
- Test: `node tools/test_world_chat.js` (60 checks; a real server + 4 Chromium phones; `SHOT=dir` saves pictures): the key and the window, two panes that fit a 915x412 screen, typing with the game's own keyboard in full screen (the window stands above the keys, the strip shows the typed text, the ✓ key sends), text / emoji / sticker reach the others, links / numbers / @names refused, swear word masked, duplicate and flood stopped, a posted lobby shows as a card with seats / fee / league / Join and its code is not in the room's data, Join -> ✓ / ✗ banner -> declined / accepted / no answer / closed, block, report, the admin list and three reporters muting, unmute.

## Y8: an ad on the FIRST click, and no social-media out-links (Y8's three replies, Nov 13)
Y8 answered three messages: (1) the publisher support: eCPM depends on the players' countries and the advertisers (nothing to change), but *"the ad placement is not good, show an ad on the first click so more players can see the ads"* and *"a huge difference between your plays and ad impressions"* (7 353 opens -> 1 670 ads); (2) the dashboard (Feedback): *"Please remove the social media out-links"* (a picture of the INBOX window); (3) the SDK team: *"no ads are showing, where do you call the ad functions?"*.
- **Why a reviewer (and most visitors) saw no ad:** the start ad needed `profile.tutDone` (a finished tutorial) and was asked at load, without a click. A new visitor - most of Y8's traffic, and a reviewer who opens the game for the first time - got nothing.
- **Now (`y8FirstFire()`):** the FIRST `click` / tap of every visit on the Y8 page starts the short `start` ad, `showAd({type: "start", name: "first-click"})` - new players too. It is asked 60 ms after the click (capture phase on `document`), so a rewarded key that was tapped first starts its own ad instead; once per visit; not for ad-free players; if the SDK is still loading the next click tries again; no ad is asked at load any more on the Y8 page (an ad is never started without a click). Every player's first click is that ad, a returning player's too (the "Gift" video window that used to come first is gone: the owner asked for one ad on entering and nothing else); `Y8.pre` keeps anybody from getting two. The Playgama / CrazyGames / GameMonetize builds, the Android app and Telegram are unchanged (their SDK decides when an ad really shows; `y8Preroll` still asks it at open there). `/admin/ads` shows `y8 | start | first-click | viewed ...`.
- **Where the game calls the ad functions** (all through `Y8.sdk.showAd`, `y8Ad()` in `index.html`): `start / first-click` (above), `start / game-start` (only the other builds that ask it at load), `next / between-matches` (the player presses a key on the result screen: after every 2nd finished match, at least 90 s after the last ad - on Y8, Telegram and the Android app (it used to be every 4th game there), whether or not the player took the ×2; the app shows none before a new player's 4th game; the Playgama / GameMonetize / Crazy builds keep their own portal pacing), and the rewarded `reward / <name>`, always started by a key the player presses, at most 10 a day: `double-win` (the "📺 ×2" key after a won match), `free-coins` (Free rewards page, the missions window), `bank-cash`, `unlock-<item>` ("watch N ads" in the shop), `try-cue`, `try-table` (try a premium cue / table for one match).
- **Telegram Mini App too:** `y8FirstFire()` also runs there when an Adsgram interstitial block is set (`Y8.startAds === "tg"`): the first click of a visit starts the start ad (`first-click`), new players too, once, not for ad-free players, none without the block. The match-start ad (after 3 games, 150 s apart) and the break between matches are unchanged. Test: `node tools/test_tg_first_click.js`.
- **"Ads are not available right now" on a portal now says why** (second line of the toast, `adNoWhy()`): `sdk-missing` (the ad SDK never loaded), `sdk-not-ready` (still loading), or what the network answered - `notReady`, `noAdPreloaded` (no ad for this player right now), `timeout`, `frequencyCapped`, `ignored`, `error · <the SDK's own message>` (the call was refused). Same word goes to `/admin/ads`. The dark screen before the toast is the ad SDK's own overlay while it asks the network; the game cannot draw or remove it. A normal (non-rewarded) ad is NOT accepted as a reward: Google's rules forbid paying for viewing anything but a rewarded ad.
- **Social-media out-links:** `NO_SOCIAL = ON_PORTAL` (Y8 and the other portal builds; the website, Telegram and the app keep them). On a portal: no Facebook / Telegram / Instagram / TikTok keys in the INBOX window - they are PAINTED into the owner's picture, so the portal pages use a clean copy of it, `IMG.ibm_bg_ns` (made by `python3 tools/make_ibm_clean.py`, needs Pillow: it repaints the strip with the picture's own surroundings, row by row, and writes the copy behind `IMG.ibm_bg`; run it again after changing the picture); a news item's "open this page" key is not shown; the "official channel" row and the "show my name in the channel" switch in Settings are hidden (`html.nosoc .chanonly`); the result screen has no "share on Facebook / X" keys.
- Test: `node tools/test_y8_build.js` (32 checks: no window and no ad at load, the first-click ad for a returning player, for one with the day's ads used up and for a new player, once, asked only once when no ad is available, none for ad-free players, INBOX / Settings / result screen without the social keys on Y8 and with them on the plain website).

## Promo video (1 minute, 1080p): `tools/promo_video/`
The owner: "make the 26-second video one minute: club, lobby, cues, tables, voice, in-game moments, gifts, avatars, chat, world chat, and an ad for the VIP cues". Everything is recorded from the REAL game (Chromium, 1920x1080) against the repo server with a cast of seven DEMO players (local accounts, not real people); nothing is mocked in the page.
- `make_voice.py` writes speech-like WAV files that Chromium plays as the "microphone" (`--use-file-for-fake-audio-capture`), so two real pages really talk in the lobby's voice room (WebRTC) and the talking rings pulse. The video has no recorded speech; the music is generated (`build_music.py`, numpy only, nothing sampled or copied).
- `lib.js` (server, cast, websocket bots that sit in a lobby, the recorded page's profile), `rec_scenes.js` (one scene per feature: `lobby club world gift shop vip`; `REC=1` records `$SP/pv/<scene>.webm` + the moments it noted in `meta.json`, without `REC` it only saves screenshots).
- `build_full.py` + `storyboard.py` cut the recordings into shots (speed-ups for typing, slow push-ins), add the caption chips (English or Uzbek: `build_full.py OUT.mp4 uz`), the VIP-cue ad with white-flash cuts and the end card, and join everything with cross-fades (ffmpeg + Pillow). `rec_match.js` / `build.py` are the first 26 s version (practice matches).
- Run: `SP=<scratch dir> node tools/promo_video/rec_scenes.js lobby club world gift shop vip` (REC=1; first `python3 tools/promo_video/make_voice.py $SP/pv/voice_N.wav N 45` for N=1..3), then `SP=<scratch dir> python3 tools/promo_video/build_full.py out.mp4 [en|uz]`, then the music: `pip install numpy`, `python3 tools/promo_video/build_music.py music.wav <total s> <VIP start s> <end card start s>` (both times are printed in `out_times.json`) and `tools/promo_video/mux_music.sh out.mp4 music.wav out_music.mp4` (about -16 LUFS).
- Things the recordings showed (not changed in the game; reported to the owner): the green "who is talking" ring of a lobby seat is hidden by the later `.lbslot.on` rule; `.lbr` rows (club member list, friends, showdown list) depend on CSS variables that only the ratings screen defines; framed avatars have percentage margins, so in wide rows (chat lines, world-chat lobby cards) they are pushed far apart.

## World chat: "Tarjima" (translate) button (the owner's idea, Nov 16; only the world chat)
- Under another player's text line a small **🌐 Tarjima** button shows the line in the reader's own game language (all 16 languages); a sticker and one's own lines have none. Pressing it asks the server, the translation appears under the line and stays while the line lives (1 hour).
- **Server:** `POST /api/world/translate {id, to}` calls **Google Cloud Translation v2** (`GOOGLE_TRANSLATE_KEY`, set by the owner on Render: never in chat; the key goes in the `X-goog-api-key` header). **No key = no button** (`GET /api/world` says `tr:false`). A line is translated once per language (cache); one player gets 8 translations per 48 s; the whole game `TRANSLATE_DAILY_CHARS` characters a day (default 12000, about 360k a month, inside Google's 500k free month; the limit shows "not available"); the translated text goes through the same swear filter; a line already in the reader's language says so (`same`). `TRANSLATE_URL` points the server at a mock in the tests.
- **Check Google's supported-languages list for `uz` before relying on it:** no source I found confirmed Uzbek for every provider. The real translation can only be checked with the owner's key.
- Needs: Render deploy (server) + `web/` + a new aab for the app. Test: `node tools/test_world_translate.js` (20 checks, mock Google, real server and phones).

## Frames of other players and the scratch window (the owner's Telegram screenshots, Nov 16)
- **Other players saw a wrong frame:** the server stored the frame with `int(b.frame, 99)` but the game has **128 frames**: every frame from #100 on (the scary `fs_*`, the VIP `vz_*`, `pp_14` / `pp_15`) reached the other players (friends list, world chat, lobby, matches) as frame **#99**, an oversized ornate one. Now `int(b.frame, 999)`. The players' own phones send their frame at every sync, so the stored wrong ones heal by themselves after a Render deploy. Test: `node server/test_frame_sync.js` (frames 0 / 99 / 100 / 127, avatar 563). **When frames are added: the server limit must stay above the number of frames.**
- **Scratch & win ("Qirib yuting") window too big:** its compact sizes (in the `max-height:460px` block) never applied (a later base rule won, and a media query cannot see the TURNED page's height), so on a phone the 3rd row of cells lay outside the window. `html.mh560 #modal ...` (the logical height, turned or not) now gives 56 px cells, no big icon, a smaller title and button. Laptops keep 90 px cells. Test: `node tools/test_scratch_fit.js` (25 checks, six screens, two languages).

## GameMonetize: the entry ad, and the 8-ball that "came back by itself" (the owner, Nov 17, GameMonetize's "Verify SDK in the game" window)
- **"This GameMonetize shows no ad":** GameMonetize's check wants to see an advertisement shown through its SDK (`sdk.showBanner()`, with `SDK_GAME_PAUSE` / `SDK_GAME_START` around it). The game's ad when it opens (`y8Preroll`) waited for `Y8.adsReady`, which the GameMonetize build sets to **false** (no rewarded video there), so no entry ad ever came; the between-match ad needs two finished matches. Now the GameMonetize build (like Playgama) asks the entry ad **once per visit, new players too, not ad-free players**, and waits up to 6 s for the SDK script if it is still loading. After every 2nd match one more (at most one per 90 s). The 📺 "watch N ads" keys answer "Ads are not available right now" there: GameMonetize has no rewarded video. Test: `node tools/test_gm_ads.js` (13 checks, mock SDK; with the old build 5 of them fail).
- **Nov 20, the owner on `cached.gamemonetize.co` (screenshots: Minigames with the toast "Ads are not available right now / SDK-NOT-READY"; a match in portrait): "no ads, and did you remove the full-screen key?"**
  - **Full screen:** yes, it was missing: `tools/build_crazy.py` removed the game's own full-screen keys (menu bar `#fsBtn`, in a match `#gFs`) for every portal except Playgama (that branch was written for CrazyGames, which brings its own). GameMonetize now takes the Playgama branch: the keys stay and hide by themselves where the frame may not go full screen (`document.fullscreenEnabled === false`).
  - **The toast** came from a 📺 "watch N ads" key (Minigames "Play again", free rewards ...): GameMonetize has **no rewarded video**, so those keys cannot work there (known, see above); the line under the toast said `sdk-not-ready`, which read like a fault, now `no-rewarded-video-here`. The entry ad and the between-match interstitial are separate (`sdk.showBanner()`).
  - **(Superseded on Nov 21, see the next bullet: the real SDK was read and the fault was found.) Whether GameMonetize really serves an ad could not be checked here:** its `sdk.js` could not be fetched from the coding sandbox (host not allowed), so the real SDK's rules (ad fill for a game that is not published yet, minimum delays, domain checks) are NOT read, unlike Playgama's Bridge. To see what the SDK does on a phone, **the end of Settings now carries `GM sdk ok|none ready ok|no asked N shown N (other event)`**: `sdk` = the script arrived, `ready` = SDK_READY came, `asked` = how many times the game called `showBanner()`, `shown` = how many times the SDK really paused the game for an ad (SDK_GAME_PAUSE), `(...)` = the name of any other event (an error). A screenshot of that line after some play says whether the problem is "no ad served" (asked > 0, shown 0) or "the SDK did not load" (sdk none).
  - Test: `node tools/test_gm_ads.js` now 16 checks (the full-screen keys, the Settings line, the why-line; with the old build script those 3 fail).
- **Nov 21, the owner: "GameMonetize cannot fail to show ads: Icy Tower, which GameMonetize did not even accept, shows them" (screenshot of Icy Tower on `cached.gamemonetize.co`: a black screen, a Google display ad and "You can skip this in 1 secs") - he was right, the fault was ours.**
  - **How it was found:** `api.gamemonetize.com` and `gamemonetize.com` were allowed in the coding sandbox's environment (Settings), the real `sdk.js` (434 KB, obfuscated: its strings are a hex-escaped table at the top) was read and run in Chromium with a fake Google IMA. The SDK carries its own jQuery (`window.jQuery = window.$ = ...`) and asks for every ad through the **bare name `$`**: `showBanner()` -> `readyPromise.then(...)` -> `$.getJSON("https://api.gamemonetize.com/datax.json", cb)` -> inside `cb` the ad request (`videoAdInstance.requestAd().then(loadAd)`); when the video ad has no fill `_onAdError` retries once with its ADX tag and then shows its own display ad (`promoVideo()`: `$("<style>")`, `$("#imaContainer").append(...)`, the black screen with the "You can skip this in N secs" button of the owner's Icy Tower picture). All of it sits inside `try { } catch (e) {}`.
  - **The fault:** `index.html` has `const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);` in its global scope. A top-level `const` is looked up BEFORE `window.$`, so the SDK's bare `$` was the game's helper: `$.getJSON` is not a function, the TypeError was swallowed by the SDK's own `catch`, **no ad was ever requested** (`asked 1 shown 0`, no error anywhere). Icy Tower has no `$` of its own, so it got its ads. The mock SDK of `tools/test_gm_ads.js` did not use `$`, so every test passed while the page showed nothing (the lesson: a mock that does not copy the real script's way of working proves nothing about it).
  - **The fix (GameMonetize build only; `index.html` and the other builds are untouched):** `tools/build_crazy.py` renames the game's helper to **`$b8`** in every inline script of the page (`tools/jsrename.py`: a small tokenizer, ~710 tokens; strings, template text, regex literals, comments and `x.$` property names are never touched; the build stops if fewer than 500 tokens were renamed or an inline handler in the markup still calls `$(`). The SDK's `$` is its own jQuery again. Cross-checked with a real parser (acorn): 710 `$` identifiers before, 0 after, 710 `$b8`, every inline script parses. No name clash in either direction between the SDK's globals (`comAPI`, `ShowAds`, `promoVideo`, `adxAds`, `isMobile`, `loadsc` ...) and the page's ~1500 top-level names.
  - **Checked on the real SDK (Chromium, fake IMA, the page served as `https://cached.gamemonetize.co/`):** the entry ad is requested (`datax.json`, then `pubads.g.doubleclick.net/gampad/ads?iu=/21739493398/ca-games-pub-5519830896693885-tag&description_url=<the page>`), `SDK_GAME_PAUSE` / `SDK_GAME_START` come around it (`asked 1 shown 1`), the SDK's overlay (`z-index 99`) is the topmost element over the whole game while the ad plays, the game is back to normal after it; with no fill, the SDK's ADX retry and its display ad (black screen, "You can skip this in 5 secs") cover the whole game too, like the Icy Tower picture. The old build on the same real SDK: `asked 1, pause 0`, not one ad request.
  - **Not proven (cannot be from here):** that GameMonetize's ad network FILLS this game (a new, unpublished game may get no video fill at first; the SDK then falls back to its display ad), and anything on a real phone. The Settings line (`GM sdk ... asked N shown N`) still tells on a phone.
  - **The SDK's own rules, for reference (read in its code):** minimum gap between two `showBanner()` calls is **130 s** on GameMonetize's own domains (`gamemonetize.com / .co`, `html5.gamemonetize.*`, y8.com and the ~3500 domains of `datax.json`) and 30 s elsewhere; a call inside the gap does nothing but fire `SDK_GAME_START` ("Just resume the game"). So `asked` can be larger than `shown` (the game's own guard is 90 s, the SDK's 130 s). The SDK loads Google's IMA (`imasdk.googleapis.com`) and `gamemonetize.com/ga.js`; an ad blocker on either makes its ready promise fail ("IMA script failed to load! Probably due to an ADBLOCKER!"). Every `showBanner()` downloads `datax.json` (~108 KB).
  - **Tests:** `node tools/test_gm_ads.js` (22 checks: the mock SDK now asks through the bare `$` like the real one, an acorn parse of the built page finds no `$` left, every screen opens, a practice match starts; with the old build script 8 fail) and **`GM_SDK_DIR=/tmp/gmsdk node tools/test_gm_real_sdk.js`** (14 checks on the real `sdk.js` + a fake IMA; skipped when the files are missing; with the old build script 5 fail). Fetch the SDK files (the host `api.gamemonetize.com` must be allowed): `mkdir -p /tmp/gmsdk && cd /tmp/gmsdk && for f in sdk.js datax.json dataxx.json dataxxx.json; do curl -sSO https://api.gamemonetize.com/$f; done`. **Re-run the real-SDK test when GameMonetize changes its SDK or when a new top-level name is added to the page.**
- **Nov 21, later the same day: the owner uploaded v2.7 and "it does not work" - the portal still serves the NOV 16 build. None of the later uploads (v2.5 / v2.6 / v2.7 at least) ever went live.**
  - **How it was seen:** `api.gamemonetize.com`, `gamemonetize.com`, `html5.gamemonetize.com / .co` and `uncached.gamemonetize.com / .co` were allowed in the coding sandbox's environment. The game's public address `https://html5.gamemonetize.com/<gameId>/` redirects to `https://html5.gamemonetize.co/<gameId>/` (the "uncached" one to `uncached.gamemonetize.co`; the Verify window loads that one). `curl -sSI https://html5.gamemonetize.co/<gameId>/index.html` gave `last-modified: Mon, 16 Nov 2026 18:50:02 GMT`, the build time of the zip sent on Nov 16 at 18:50 UTC (35 203 640 bytes, 1502 files); the live page still has the old `const $ = id`, `sdk-not-ready`, no `fsBtn`, no `GM sdk` line. Its 1501 pictures are all there (200); 8 of the 29 new cue pictures of v2.5+ that were probed are 404: **nothing of v2.5+ was extracted, not even partly.** So the toast "SDK-NOT-READY" in the owner's Nov 20 and Nov 21 screenshots came from this old build: the fixes of v2.5 .. v2.7 never ran on his phone.
  - **Why the uploads do not apply is NOT known.** Facts: the Nov 16 zip (35.2 MB, 1502 files) was applied; v2.5+ are 36.07 MB / 1531 files (the 29 new cues); at 13:43 the owner's page showed the upload at 55% (36.1 MB), at 13:44 an empty upload box with the Verify window already open (he may have pressed Verify before the upload ended, or never pressed "Save Changes"). Which of these it is was not proven.
  - **Check that an upload went live before judging a screenshot:** `curl -sSI https://html5.gamemonetize.co/<gameId>/index.html | grep -i last-modified` (= the build time of the zip) and `curl -s https://html5.gamemonetize.co/<gameId>/index.html | grep -c '\$b8'` (the sandbox needs those hosts allowed). A watcher for "the file changed": poll that Last-Modified.
  - **How Verify works (read in `sdk.js`):** the SDK posts `{"type":"SDK_IMPLEMENTED"}` to `window.parent` when a VIDEO ad has played to its end (`CONTENT_RESUME_REQUESTED`); the no-fill display ad ("You can skip this in N secs") does not send it. On a phone the browser may refuse an unmuted video before any tap (the entry ad comes before a tap): the SDK then falls back to the display ad. If Verify does not pass after the new build is live, look there first (the entry ad could wait for the first tap, as the Y8 build does).
  - GameMonetize's own SDK documentation is on GitHub (the owner sent screenshots of the README): `SDK_OPTIONS {gameId, onEvent}` + the loader snippet, `sdk.showBanner()`, mute/pause at `SDK_GAME_PAUSE`, resume at `SDK_GAME_START`: the game does exactly that (it mutes; the ads come at the menu / result screen, where there is nothing to pause).
- **Nov 21, the cause found: big zips are not applied, small ones are.** A 374-byte test zip (one page that says "UPLOAD TEST 1") went live within minutes (Last-Modified = the zip's file time, 09:20:32 GMT), the old 1501 pictures stayed on the host: **the portal unpacks a zip OVER the files already there.** The full zips of v2.5 - v2.7 (36 MB, 1531 files) were never applied (the Nov 16 zip with 35.2 MB / 1502 files had been). The limit (size, file count or time) is not known. **So send GameMonetize a DELTA zip:** `python3 tools/gm_delta.py <full gamemonetize zip> <out zip>` asks the host (HEAD for every `img/<hash>.webp`; pictures are named by content hash) and writes index.html + only the pictures the host lacks (v2.7: 29 pictures, 4.2 MB, 30 files). The sandbox needs `html5.gamemonetize.co` allowed. After the owner's upload: `curl -sSI https://html5.gamemonetize.co/<gameId>/index.html | grep -i last-modified` and `grep -c '\$b8'` on it. (The owner's page said "The game was updated successfully" after Save Changes even when no file had changed: that banner is about the form only.)
- **Nov 21, it went live: a DELTA zip (`bilyard8-gamemonetize-v27-d7500c8-delta2.zip` content: index.html 3.9 MB + 647 pictures, 4.3 MB) was applied; the full 36 MB zips never were.** Checked on the host: index.html has `const $b8`, `no-rewarded-video-here`, `GM sdk`, `fsBtn`, `lang="en"`; all 2148 pictures it refers to answer 200. **The portal applies an upload LATE: the 374-byte test zip after about 5 minutes, delta2 after 10 - 15 minutes** (the owner's screenshots 10 minutes after the upload still showed the old page; he was told twice to upload again). So: after an upload wait 15 minutes and check `Last-Modified` before calling it failed. The theory "a second dot in the zip's file name (`v2.7`) is refused" was wrong (the dotted name was applied). The host's index.html is 4.6 KB longer than the zip's: Cloudflare's font optimisation rewrites the Google Fonts `<link>` into an inline `@font-face` style, nothing of ours. The unique zip names keep "v27" style (no dot) from now on only where it helps; the rule in CLAUDE.md (portal + version + commit) stays.
